([ChronoAIProject/Ornn](https://github.com/ChronoAIProject/Ornn/tree/HEAD/skills/chrono-ai-service-manual) · ★20 · 2 copies)
- **chrono-ai-weekly-report**: Pulls a GitHub account's issues and pull requests for the week, analyzes what shipped, what is in progress, and what is blocked with real workload metrics, then renders a Lark interactive card with hyperlinked rows and a metrics table, or a markdown report. The agent runs the whole flow itself using its own NyxID-brokered tools. ([ChronoAIProject/nyx-skills](https://github.com/ChronoAIProject/nyx-skills/tree/HEAD/chrono-ai-weekly-report))
- **chrono-cd**: Deploy a user's application to the ChronoAI cluster with chrono-cd through NyxID, either with the CLI (`nyxid proxy request chrono-cd ...`) or, for agents without a shell such as the NyxID Assistant and NyxBot on Telegram or Lark, with their NyxID proxy request tool (service `chrono-cd`, method, path, body). Covers the whole flow an agent runs for the user - check the account and quota, pick a personal or organization scope, write the app spec (one or more ghcr.io images with ports, routes, env, config files, volumes and an optional MongoDB add-on), create or update the app, set its secrets,… ([ChronoAIProject/nyx-skills](https://github.com/ChronoAIProject/nyx-skills/tree/HEAD/chrono-cd))

- **chronoroot2-segmentation-eval**: Evaluates deep learning models for simultaneous multi-organ segmentation in 2D plant images. It probes the model's ability to accurately delineate root systems and aerial parts across different plant species and light conditions, while preserving structural fidelity for downstream phenotypic analysis. Use when the user wants to benchmark on Arabidopsis thaliana 2D phenotyping dataset, Tomato 2D phenotyping dataset, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports Dice coefficient. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/chronoroot2-segmentation-eval) · ★3)
- **chronos**: Set up, check, pause, or remove Chronos on Windows and explain its privacy controls. Use when the user mentions Chronos or reports Codex lag, resource degradation, SQLite growth, or unusual token/quota use. Diagnostics require authorization; ordinary repository work does not trigger inspection. ([FaxanFM/chronos](https://github.com/FaxanFM/chronos/tree/HEAD/plugins/chronos/skills/chronos) · ★6)
- **chronos**: **Greek:** χρόνος (chronos) — "time" ([TylerGarlick/abraxas](https://github.com/TylerGarlick/abraxas/tree/HEAD/skills/chronos) · ★6)
